# Third-party notices

Generated by `node .github/scripts/generate-notices.mjs` from the committed lockfiles and the Windows dependency graph. Regenerate after dependency changes using a locked installation (`npm ci`). This inventory intentionally includes Rust build dependencies as well as runtime dependencies; not every listed crate is shipped in the final executable.

Vellora was originally built as OpenFlow. The project's original attribution identifies OpenWhispr (https://github.com/OpenWhispr/openwhispr) and FreeFlow (https://github.com/zachlatta/freeflow) as product/workflow references. The owner reports development solely with AI agents. The current logo was generated through Codex's built-in OpenAI image generation tool on October 4, 2026; its text prompts, image inputs and asset hashes are recorded in docs/ARTWORK_PROVENANCE.md. Earlier unverified artwork remains only in private historical material; see docs/LICENSING_REVIEW.md for the clean-history publication plan and rights limitations.

Upstream license declarations and available license/notice texts are reproduced below. Vellora's MIT license does not replace any dependency's license. For MPL-2.0 components, upstream source is available from the crate source links in the inventory. A public binary release requires a final license review, including any listed gaps. This file, Vellora's LICENSE and THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES_NATIVE.md are included as installer resources; the supplemental file covers external Windows installer/loader components outside the Cargo/npm inventory.

## Verified upstream notices recovered from exact source commits

Some crate packages omit their workspace license files. Their published `.cargo_vcs_info.json` identifies the exact source commit used below. Dual-licensed MIT/Apache packages use the MIT option in these recovered notices. For `selectors`, the MPL-2.0 header is preserved from the exact upstream source file and the license text comes from Mozilla, its license steward.

- alloc-stdlib 0.2.2, commit `6032b6a9b20e03737135c55a0270ccffcc1438ef`: [notice source](https://github.com/dropbox/rust-alloc-no-stdlib/blob/6032b6a9b20e03737135c55a0270ccffcc1438ef/LICENSE).
- clipboard-win 5.4.1, commit `3b27cf2bfd1adcfa6e0264eb51c1025ddaf0f342`: [notice source](https://github.com/DoumanAsh/clipboard-win/blob/3b27cf2bfd1adcfa6e0264eb51c1025ddaf0f342/LICENSE).
- dasp_sample 0.11.0, commit `97c3bb9b2363c0b46ac1633858bf1054fd02a980`: [notice source](https://github.com/rustaudio/sample/blob/97c3bb9b2363c0b46ac1633858bf1054fd02a980/LICENSE-MIT).
- selectors 0.36.1, commit `635e1a19d02960588a00e189bd4bd5bdb150ec3d`: [notice source](https://www.mozilla.org/media/MPL/2.0/index.txt), [notice source](https://github.com/servo/stylo/blob/635e1a19d02960588a00e189bd4bd5bdb150ec3d/selectors/lib.rs).
- tauri-plugin 2.6.2, commit `499df79be65ef8c0670abc0207cd9e37b55d8491`: [notice source](https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri/blob/499df79be65ef8c0670abc0207cd9e37b55d8491/LICENSE_MIT).
- unic-char-property 0.9.0, commit `5878605364af97a3358368a6eaef02104af2e016`: [notice source](https://github.com/open-i18n/rust-unic/blob/5878605364af97a3358368a6eaef02104af2e016/LICENSE-MIT), [notice source](https://github.com/open-i18n/rust-unic/blob/5878605364af97a3358368a6eaef02104af2e016/COPYRIGHT.md).

## Notice coverage

Every listed package has an included license or notice text, either from its published archive or the verified upstream sources above. This verifies notice-file coverage, not every possible distribution obligation; a final distribution license review is still required.
